Every verb that outlives its effect already hands its process away first (=instance/start= detaches and =unref()=s its supervisor, =start.js:23-28=; =instance/run= awaits its children, =run.js:24=); =view.emit= resolves on =waitUntilExit=, =console.log= is synchronous — nothing in flight is cut.

** M12 — the captured face and =viva home <yap>=
The first target, beef's sentence: /"when i $viva home yap yap yap yap yap. then i want the effect triggering interactive, full shell chrome, runtime started if instance not already live …, logged in with credentials …, lighthouse connected, terminals and daemons loaded, mode harness connected."/ After M4–M11 the path is already open. =/home= is a reference edge whose row is the identity's HOMED mode; the effect is =reference(ctx, row, home)= → =session.open= → =boot(row, first)= → =turn(first)=. What this milestone adds is the surface, cut to the comp (=** the comp=): the boot gate, then the captured face — the transcript in scrollback, the slot, the live turn, the status row and the dock. It is built from five parts that the chrome face (M13) reuses. anima's =Dock.svelte= is the dock; its turns are =thread.$turns= (=client/entities/thread/dossier.js:27=), the user line is echoed until its id lands, ctrl-c stops the activity through its stdin (=client/entities/activity.js:56 owed=), and esc ends the session.

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/store.js :mkdirp yes
import { useEffect, useState } from "@vivalence/sheets";

export const useStore = (store, initial = null) => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(() => store?.get?.() ?? initial);
  useEffect(() => (store ? store.subscribe(setValue) : undefined), [store]);
  return value;
};

export const transcript = (turns, notes, echo) => {
  const shown = echo && !turns.some((turn) => turn.id === echo.id) ? [...turns, echo] : turns;
  return [...shown, ...notes].sort((a, b) => String(a.createdAt).localeCompare(String(b.createdAt)));
};
#+END_SRC

The boot gate is the elevation from verb to session (=** the comp=, pass 2): the five step rows, the hosted runtime's log tail, and the sign-in form in place when no credentials answer. It is the first thing the capture paints and it collapses to nothing when the session is seated — /"the boot summary line after the gate"/ is cut (handoff doc). =SignIn= is held through the capture's own =cut.hold= (M3), so its form owns the input while the gate waits, and esc leaves through the one teardown:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Boot.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, Form, PasswordInput, React, Tasks, Text, TextInput, theme, useInput } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { useStore } from "./store.js";

export function Boot({ session, slot, holding }) {
  const steps = useStore(session.$boot, []) ?? [];
  const tail = useStore(session.$tail, []) ?? [];
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Tasks items={steps} />
      {tail.length ? <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>{tail.map((line, at) => <Text key={at} color={theme.dim}>  {line}</Text>)}</Box> : null}
      {slot ? <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>{slot}</Box> : null}
      <Text color={theme.dim}>› {holding ? "signing in…" : "booting…"}</Text>
      <Text color={theme.dim}>esc leave</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}

export function SignIn({ origin, error, controller }) {
  useInput((_, key) => {
    if (key.escape) controller.kill("SIGTERM", "esc");
  });
  const done = ({ values }) => {
    if (!values.username || !values.password) return;
    controller.stdout.close(values);
  };
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Text>
        <Text bold>sign in</Text>
        <Text color={theme.dim}> · no VIVA_CLIENT_GHOST_CREDENTIALS_FILE — {origin}</Text>
      </Text>
      <Form
        pages={[{ title: "sign in", fields: [{ name: "username", label: "username", input: TextInput }, { name: "password", label: "password", input: PasswordInput }] }]}
        actions={["sign in"]}
        done={done}
      />
      {error ? <Text color={theme.bad}>✕ {error}</Text> : null}
      <Text color={theme.dim}>⏎ next · tab move · esc leave</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

=Tasks= needs the comp's fourth state for the signed-out lighthouse: =warn= paints =!= in the warn colour (hunk under =** the comp=, pass 2). A sign-in with a blank field does not submit — the form's =done= refuses it and the row stays where it is; the comp's =✕ username and password are both needed= line is the same refusal said aloud, QUESTIONED whether a line is wanted where the caret already says it.

A turn, a note, the leave line. The mode's slug after the brand mark, never "assistant"; one tool per glyph — =◐ name= running, =▸ name= done, =✕ name= failed (a =tool_result= part with =is_error= against the call's id). A note is a transcript line the session writes (=── seated= · =── reseated= · =── closed= · =▪ stopped=), dim, with no role:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Turn.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, theme } from "@vivalence/sheets";

const text = (turn) => (turn?.parts ?? []).filter((part) => part?.type === "text").map((part) => part.text).join("");
const failed = (turn) => new Set((turn?.parts ?? []).filter((part) => part?.type === "tool_result" && part.is_error).map((part) => part.tool_use_id));
const tools = (turn) => (turn?.parts ?? []).filter((part) => part?.type === "tool_use").map((part) => ({ name: part.name, id: part.id }));

function Tool({ name, running, broken }) {
  if (broken) return <Text color={theme.bad}>  ✕ {name}</Text>;
  return running ? <Text color={theme.accent}>  ◐ {name}</Text> : <Text color={theme.dim}>  ▸ {name}</Text>;
}

export function Turn({ turn, mode, live = false }) {
  if (turn.note) return <Text color={theme.dim}>{turn.note}</Text>;
  const spoken = text(turn);
  const called = tools(turn);
  const broken = failed(turn);
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" marginBottom={live ? 0 : 1}>
      {turn.role === "user" ? <Text color={theme.accent} bold>you</Text> : (
        <Text>
          <Text color={theme.brand} bold>{theme.glyph.mark} </Text>
          <Text bold>{mode ?? turn.role}</Text>
          {called.map((tool, index) => <Tool key={tool.id ?? index} name={tool.name} running={live && index === called.length - 1 && !broken.has(tool.id)} broken={broken.has(tool.id)} />)}
        </Text>
      )}
      {spoken ? <Text>{spoken}</Text> : null}
    </Box>
  );
}

export function Left({ left, turns, instance, running }) {
  return <Text color={theme.dim}>{theme.glyph.mark} left {left} · {turns} turn{turns === 1 ? "" : "s"} · {instance} {running ? "still running" : "stopped"}</Text>;
}
#+END_SRC

The status row, following the comp's ladder. The =✦= colour is the standing (=session.$standing=: =verified= ok · =checking= / =connecting= accent · =signed out= dim; =unreachable= bad is not read today — =** the comp=, lacks). At 120 columns it adds origin, launch and the buffer index; below that it keeps instance · identity · seat and clips the terminal label first. The one live state sits at the right edge: =● streaming= · =✕ error= · =◐ connecting= · =verified= from 120 cols. =inverse= is the crown (M13), whose idle word is =chrome=:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Status.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, theme, useStdout } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { useStore } from "./store.js";

const MARK = { verified: "ok", checking: "accent", connecting: "accent", unreachable: "bad", "signed out": "dim" };

export function Status({ session, inverse = false }) {
  const { stdout } = useStdout();
  const wide = (stdout.columns ?? 80) >= 120;
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const buffer = useStore(terminal?.$buffer);
  const buffers = useStore(thread?.$buffers, []) ?? [];
  const streaming = useStore(session.$live);
  const error = useStore(session.$error);
  const standing = useStore(session.$standing, "connecting");
  const identity = session.held?.lighthouse.identity?.slug ?? null;
  const seat = session.seated ? `${session.seated.daemon} › ${wide ? `${session.seated.type}/` : ""}${session.seated.slug}` : "nothing seated";
  const parts = [session.instance, identity ?? "—", seat];
  if (wide && session.held) parts.push(session.held.origin, session.hosting.launch, `⚎ ${buffers.indexOf(buffer) + 1}/${buffers.length}`);
  const label = terminal?.trait?.LABELED;
  if (label) parts.push(label);
  const state = streaming ? [theme.accent, "● streaming"] : error ? [theme.bad, "✕ error"] : standing === "connecting" || standing === "checking" ? [theme.accent, `◐ ${standing}`] : inverse ? [undefined, "chrome"] : wide && standing === "verified" ? [theme.ok, "verified"] : null;
  return (
    <Box justifyContent="space-between">
      <Text inverse={inverse} wrap="truncate-end">
        <Text color={theme[MARK[standing] ?? "dim"]}>{inverse ? " " : ""}{theme.glyph.mark} </Text>
        <Text bold>{parts[0]}</Text>
        <Text color={theme.dim}> · {parts.slice(1).join(" · ")}</Text>
      </Text>
      {state ? <Text inverse={inverse} color={state[0]}>{state[1]} </Text> : null}
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

The dock owns the keys that both faces share: ⏎ sends, ctrl-c stops what runs (and leaves when nothing runs), ctrl-f swaps the face, and esc leaves from captured or steps back to captured from chrome. The input stays open while a turn streams — a line sent then is refused by the session (=a turn is still streaming — ctrl-c stops it=, on =$error=), never queued. Every key stands down while a held component owns the slot (=live=, M3). No placeholder (cut, handoff doc):

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Dock.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, TextInput, theme, useInput, useRef, useState } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { owed, roster } from "../../client/mod.js";
import { useStore } from "./store.js";

const HINTS = {
  captured: "⏎ send · esc leave · ctrl-c leave · ctrl-f chrome",
  chrome: "⏎ send · esc captured · ctrl-t terminal · ctrl-o mode · ctrl-f",
  streaming: "ctrl-c stop · esc leave",
};

export function Dock({ session, controller, holding, face, shift }) {
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const activity = React.useMemo(() => roster(thread), [thread]);
  const activities = useStore(activity, []) ?? [];
  const streaming = useStore(session.$live);
  const [line, setLine] = useState("");
  const signalled = useRef(new Set()).current;
  const running = activities.some((row) => row.status === "RUNNING");

  useInput(
    (input, key) => {
      if (key.ctrl && input === "f") return shift(face === "chrome" ? "captured" : "chrome");
      if (key.escape) return face === "chrome" ? shift("captured") : controller.kill("SIGTERM", "esc");
      if (!(key.ctrl && input === "c")) return;
      const owing = owed("SIGTERM", activities, signalled);
      if (!owing.length) return controller.kill("SIGTERM", "ctrl-c");
      for (const row of owing) row.stdin.SIGTERM("user pressed stop");
      session.note("  ▪ stopped");
    },
    { isActive: !holding },
  );

  const submit = (typed) => {
    if (!typed.trim()) return;
    setLine("");
    session.more(typed);
  };

  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Box>
        <Text color={holding ? theme.dim : theme.accent}>› </Text>
        <TextInput value={line} onChange={setLine} onSubmit={submit} isDisabled={holding} prompt="" />
      </Box>
      {holding ? null : <Text color={theme.dim}>{running || streaming ? HINTS.streaming : HINTS[face]}</Text>}
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Captured.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Static, Text, theme } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { transcript, useStore } from "./store.js";
import { Boot } from "./Boot.jsx";
import { Turn } from "./Turn.jsx";
import { Status } from "./Status.jsx";
import { Dock } from "./Dock.jsx";

export function Captured({ session, controller, slot, live: holding, face, shift }) {
  const booting = useStore(session.$boot);
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const turns = useStore(thread?.$turns, []) ?? [];
  const notes = useStore(session.$notes, []) ?? [];
  const streaming = useStore(session.$live);
  const echo = useStore(session.$echo);
  const error = useStore(session.$error);
  const mode = session.seated?.slug;
  if (booting) return <Boot session={session} slot={slot} holding={holding} />;
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Static key={thread?.id ?? "none"} items={transcript(turns, notes, echo)}>{(turn) => <Turn key={turn.id} turn={turn} mode={mode} />}</Static>
      {slot ? <Box flexDirection="column" marginBottom={1}>{slot}</Box> : null}
      {streaming ? <Turn turn={streaming} mode={mode} live /> : null}
      {error ? <Text><Text color={theme.bad}>✕ error: </Text>{error}</Text> : null}
      <Status session={session} />
      <Dock session={session} controller={controller} holding={holding} face={face} shift={shift} />
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

=Static= commits each turn to scrollback once, so the transcript IS the scrollback. It is append-only by its index, so it is KEYED by the thread: a reseat or a terminal switch remounts it and the new thread's turns print whole, the session's notes among them in time order (=transcript=, =store.js=). Leaving chrome remounts =Captured= in the same way, which re-prints the thread the alternate screen took with it (the comp's =setFace= flush). The status row sits UNDER the transcript, over the input, because inline =Static= owns everything above the live area (=tui.md= §6). The first line of the screen belongs only to the chrome (F12 RULED by the comp: captured inline, chrome fullscreen). The =owed= ledger is a ref, not a per-render =Set=. =roster(thread)= is the client's computed over the daemon's activity store; =owed(signal, rows, sent)= filters the rows the machine lets move, once each — anima's =stop.svelte.js:41= verbatim. The slot is where a typed command paints: =instance/doctor= renders its =Effect= above the live turn and stays until the next turn clears it (=session.turn= → =capture.clear()=, M11).

The verb is M4's =reference= with M9's =home=; nothing opens here. The first walk, captured, at 80 columns — the gate, then the session:

#+BEGIN_EXAMPLE
~ $ viva home what did i get wrong yesterday
● instance    localhost · lock ALIVE pid 51200 · joined
● lighthouse  http://localhost:2503 · verified · beef
◐ daemons     mounting
○ terminal
○ harness
› booting…
esc leave
#+END_EXAMPLE

#+BEGIN_EXAMPLE
~ $ viva home what did i get wrong yesterday
── seated · hello › chaosmonkey/oracle · new thread
you
what did i get wrong yesterday

✦ oracle  ◐ viva_doctor
You asked about the subjunctive after 'penso che' — twice you wrote …
✦ localhost · beef · hello › oracle                              ● streaming
› _
ctrl-c stop · esc leave
#+END_EXAMPLE

On esc, one dim line stays in scrollback: =✦ left oracle · 1 turn · localhost still running=. The localhost identity carries no HOMED trait at signup, so the first =viva home= on a fresh localhost answers through the yap screen (M4: no home → search only) until a HOMED writer exists — deferred; the walk seeds the row's =trait.HOMED= by hand (=~/.viva/credentials.json=, the file is the operator's) and the testimony names that step.

** M13 — the chrome face, and terminals · modes · buffers on the trajectory
beef: /"for sure we can add a table somewhere to open and navigate and crud multiple terminals. trivy. we use the same ways and methods that anima uses."/ · /"maybe inline and interactive? yes. ok. inline and interactive. interactive rendering the vivalence chrome and multiple terminals."/ · 10-06: /"apply the design solutions to our cut captured adn chrome. injest them into the trajectory vector."/ The milestone was postponed (/"that comes later"/); the comp designs it, so it returns as designed, after M12. It has two halves. The chrome face (=Crowned=, fullscreen) is the crown, the spine (anima's terminal → mode → thread, as a guided form), the stage and the transcript tail, over M12's dock. The session verbs are opened on the trajectory, so a typed =terminal/new flashcards ripassiamo= in the dock and a =viva terminal/new …= from zsh meet the same edge. anima's verbs are the store's (=stores/terminals.js:22-44= =create · activate · remove=) plus =terminal.seat= (=entities/terminal.js:51=) and =createBuffer= (=panels/c/cards/Thread.svelte:4-19=). A buffer's view is a bundled svelte APPLICATION, which a terminal cannot run (no DOM), so the stage shows the buffer's =data= until a TUI view kind exists (=** the comp=, lacks).

The terminals table is ONE component in two places: the spine's terminal step (24 cols, two lines a row) and the slot in captured (=viva terminals=, one line a row), held through =cut.hold=. No header count, no hint row, no ids (cut, handoff doc). Keys: ↑↓ j k move · ⏎ picks · n opens a new one · x closes one · esc closes the table:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Terminals.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, theme, useInput, useState } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { useStore } from "./store.js";

export const label = (terminal) => terminal.trait?.LABELED ?? terminal.$thread.get()?.mode?.slug ?? "new";
export const seat = (terminal) => {
  const thread = terminal.$thread.get();
  return thread?.mode ? `${thread.daemon.slug} › ${thread.mode.type}/${thread.mode.slug}` : "no mode yet";
};

export function Terminals({ session, narrow = false, controller, done = () => controller?.stdout.close() }) {
  const terminals = useStore(session.held.terminals.$entities, []) ?? [];
  const active = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(Math.max(0, terminals.indexOf(active)));
  useInput((input, key) => {
    if (key.upArrow || input === "k") return setIndex(Math.max(0, index - 1));
    if (key.downArrow || input === "j") return setIndex(Math.min(terminals.length, index + 1));
    if (key.escape) return done();
    if (input === "n" || (key.return && index === terminals.length)) return session.open().then(done);
    if (input === "x" && terminals[index]) return session.drop(terminals[index]);
    if (key.return) return (session.activate(terminals[index]), done());
  });
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {terminals.map((terminal, at) => {
        const turns = terminal.$thread.get()?.$turns.get().length ?? 0;
        return (
          <Box key={terminal.id} flexDirection={narrow ? "column" : "row"}>
            <Text wrap="truncate-end">
              <Text color={at === index ? theme.accent : undefined}>{at === index ? "› " : "  "}</Text>
              <Text color={terminal === active ? theme.ok : theme.dim}>{terminal === active ? "● " : "○ "}</Text>
              <Text color={at === index ? theme.accent : undefined}>{narrow ? label(terminal) : label(terminal).padEnd(30)}</Text>
            </Text>
            <Text color={theme.dim} wrap="truncate-end">{narrow ? "    " : "  "}{seat(terminal)} · {turns} turn{turns === 1 ? "" : "s"}</Text>
          </Box>
        );
      })}
      <Text color={index === terminals.length ? theme.accent : theme.dim}>{index === terminals.length ? "› " : "  "}+ terminal</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

The spine. Its auto-advance — a picked terminal with a mode lands on the thread step, a new terminal opens the mode step, a seated mode lands on thread — is the comp's and the handoff doc's open question 5 (/"The interaction model was explicitly paused once … Confirm it before implementing it in Ink"/): tangled as designed, QUESTIONED until beef confirms:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Spine.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, theme, useInput, useState } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { useStore } from "./store.js";
import { Terminals, label } from "./Terminals.jsx";

const clock = (iso) => (iso ? new Date(iso).toTimeString().slice(0, 5) : "—");
const inFlight = (turn) => (turn?.parts ?? []).filter((part) => part?.type === "tool_use").at(-1)?.name ?? null;

function Step({ name, open, value }) {
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" marginBottom={open ? 0 : 1}>
      <Text>
        <Text color={open ? theme.accent : theme.ok}>{open ? theme.glyph.active : theme.glyph.done} </Text>
        <Text color={open ? theme.accent : theme.dim} bold={open}>{name}</Text>
      </Text>
      {value ? <Text wrap="truncate-end">{value}</Text> : null}
    </Box>
  );
}

function TerminalStep({ session }) {
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Step name="terminal" open />
      <Terminals session={session} narrow done={() => session.$step.set(session.seated ? "thread" : "mode")} />
    </Box>
  );
}

function Modes({ session }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const all = session.rows();
  const hits = all.filter((row) => !query || `${row.daemon} ${row.type}/${row.slug}`.includes(query));
  useInput((input, key) => {
    if (key.upArrow) return setIndex(Math.max(0, index - 1));
    if (key.downArrow) return setIndex(Math.min(hits.length - 1, index + 1));
    if (key.escape) return session.$step.set(session.seated ? "thread" : "terminal");
    if (key.backspace || key.delete) return (setQuery(query.slice(0, -1)), setIndex(0));
    if (key.return) return hits[index] && session.seat(hits[index]);
    if (input && !key.ctrl) return (setQuery(query + input), setIndex(0));
  });
  const daemons = [...new Set(hits.map((row) => row.daemon))];
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Text>
        <Text color={theme.accent}>{theme.glyph.active} </Text>
        <Text color={theme.accent} bold>mode</Text>
        <Text>  {query}</Text>
        <Text color={theme.accent}>▌</Text>
      </Text>
      {daemons.map((daemon) => (
        <Box key={daemon} flexDirection="column">
          <Text color={theme.dim}>  {daemon}</Text>
          {hits.filter((row) => row.daemon === daemon).map((row) => (
            <Text key={`${row.type}/${row.slug}`} color={hits[index] === row ? theme.accent : undefined} wrap="truncate-end">
              {hits[index] === row ? "  › " : "    "}{row.type}/{row.slug}
            </Text>
          ))}
        </Box>
      ))}
      <Text color={theme.dim}>  {hits.length ? `${hits.length} of ${all.length}${query ? " · ⌫ widen" : ""}` : "no mode matches"}</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}

function Thread({ session }) {
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const turns = useStore(thread?.$turns, []) ?? [];
  const buffer = useStore(terminal?.$buffer);
  const buffers = useStore(thread?.$buffers, []) ?? [];
  const streaming = useStore(session.$live);
  const error = useStore(session.$error);
  const tool = inFlight(streaming);
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      <Step name="thread" open />
      <Text wrap="truncate-end">
        <Text color={streaming ? theme.accent : error ? theme.bad : theme.dim}>{streaming ? "● streaming" : error ? "✕ error" : "○ idle"}</Text>
        {tool ? <Text color={theme.dim}> ◐ {tool}</Text> : null}
      </Text>
      <Text wrap="truncate-end">{turns.length} turn{turns.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}<Text color={theme.dim}> · since {clock(thread?.createdAt)}</Text></Text>
      <Text wrap="truncate-end">{buffers.length ? `buffer #${buffers.indexOf(buffer) + 1}/${buffers.length}` : "empty buffer"}<Text color={theme.dim}>{buffer?.phase ? ` · ⚎ ${buffer.phase}` : ""}</Text></Text>
    </Box>
  );
}

const HINT = { terminal: "↑↓ · ⏎ pick · n new · x close", mode: "type · ↑↓ · ⏎ seat · esc", thread: "ctrl-t terminal · ctrl-o mode" };

export function Spine({ session, width }) {
  const step = useStore(session.$step);
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const seat = session.seated ? `${session.seated.daemon} › ${session.seated.type}/${session.seated.slug}` : null;
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" width={width} justifyContent="space-between" paddingTop={1}>
      <Box flexDirection="column">
        {step === "terminal" ? <TerminalStep session={session} /> : <Step name="terminal" value={terminal ? label(terminal) : "—"} />}
        {step === "mode" ? <Modes session={session} /> : step === "terminal" ? <Text color={theme.dim}>{theme.glyph.pending} mode</Text> : <Step name="mode" value={seat} />}
        {step === "thread" ? <Thread session={session} /> : <Text color={theme.dim}>{theme.glyph.pending} thread</Text>}
      </Box>
      <Text color={theme.dim} wrap="truncate-end">{HINT[step]}</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

The stage shows the active buffer after the first turn: its head, then its data as =set: k=v · …=. The transcript in chrome is a tail, not =Static=: the alternate screen keeps no scrollback, so the box clips from the top (=overflow="hidden"= + =justifyContent="flex-end"=). The crown is M12's status row inverted. Under 100 columns the tabs replace the spine, and an open step takes the body:

#+BEGIN_SRC jsx :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/faces/Crowned.jsx :mkdirp yes
import { Box, React, Text, theme, useInput, useStdout } from "@vivalence/sheets";
import { transcript, useStore } from "./store.js";
import { Boot } from "./Boot.jsx";
import { Turn } from "./Turn.jsx";
import { Status } from "./Status.jsx";
import { Dock } from "./Dock.jsx";
import { Spine } from "./Spine.jsx";
import { label } from "./Terminals.jsx";

function Stage({ session }) {
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const turns = useStore(thread?.$turns, []) ?? [];
  const buffer = useStore(terminal?.$buffer);
  const buffers = useStore(thread?.$buffers, []) ?? [];
  const data = useStore(buffer?.$data, {}) ?? {};
  if (!turns.length || !buffer) return null;
  const fields = Object.entries(data).map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${typeof value === "object" ? JSON.stringify(value) : value}`);
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderTop={false} borderLeft={false} borderRight={false} borderColor={theme.dim}>
      <Text>
        <Text bold>{session.seated?.slug}</Text>
        <Text color={theme.dim}>  · buffer #{buffers.indexOf(buffer) + 1} of {buffers.length} · </Text>
        <Text color={theme.ok}>ACTIVE</Text>
      </Text>
      {fields.length ? <Text color={theme.dim} wrap="truncate-end">  set: {fields.join(" · ")}</Text> : null}
    </Box>
  );
}

function Tabs({ session }) {
  const terminals = useStore(session.held.terminals.$entities, []) ?? [];
  const active = useStore(session.$terminal);
  return (
    <Box justifyContent="space-between">
      <Text wrap="truncate-end">
        {terminals.map((terminal) => (
          <Text key={terminal.id} color={terminal === active ? undefined : theme.dim} bold={terminal === active}>
            <Text color={terminal === active ? theme.ok : theme.dim}>{terminal === active ? "● " : "○ "}</Text>
            {label(terminal).slice(0, 16)}{"  "}
          </Text>
        ))}
        <Text color={theme.dim}>+</Text>
      </Text>
      <Text color={theme.dim}>{session.seated ? `${session.seated.daemon} › ${session.seated.slug}` : ""}</Text>
    </Box>
  );
}

function Transcript({ session, slot }) {
  const terminal = useStore(session.$terminal);
  const thread = useStore(terminal?.$thread);
  const turns = useStore(thread?.$turns, []) ?? [];
  const notes = useStore(session.$notes, []) ?? [];
  const streaming = useStore(session.$live);
  const echo = useStore(session.$echo);
  const error = useStore(session.$error);
  const mode = session.seated?.slug;
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" flexGrow={1} overflow="hidden" justifyContent="flex-end">
      {transcript(turns, notes, echo).map((turn) => <Turn key={turn.id} turn={turn} mode={mode} />)}
      {slot ? <Box flexDirection="column" marginBottom={1}>{slot}</Box> : null}
      {streaming ? <Turn turn={streaming} mode={mode} live /> : null}
      {error ? <Text><Text color={theme.bad}>✕ error: </Text>{error}</Text> : null}
    </Box>
  );
}

export function Crowned({ session, controller, slot, live: holding, face, shift }) {
  const { stdout } = useStdout();
  const cols = stdout.columns ?? 80;
  const step = useStore(session.$step);
  const booting = useStore(session.$boot);
  const open = step !== "thread";
  useInput(
    (input, key) => {
      if (key.ctrl && input === "t") return session.$step.set("terminal");
      if (key.ctrl && input === "o") return session.$step.set("mode");
    },
    { isActive: !holding && !open },
  );
  const body = (
    <Box flexDirection="column" flexGrow={1}>
      <Stage session={session} />
      <Transcript session={session} slot={slot} />
    </Box>
  );
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" height={(stdout.rows ?? 24) - 1}>
      <Status session={session} inverse />
      {booting ? <Box flexGrow={1} paddingTop={1}><Boot session={session} slot={slot} holding={holding} /></Box> : cols >= 100 ? (
        <Box flexGrow={1}>
          <Spine session={session} width={24} />
          <Box borderStyle="single" borderTop={false} borderRight={false} borderBottom={false} borderColor={theme.dim} paddingLeft={1} flexGrow={1}>{body}</Box>
        </Box>
      ) : open ? <Box flexGrow={1}><Spine session={session} width={cols} /></Box> : (
        <Box flexDirection="column" flexGrow={1}>
          <Tabs session={session} />
          {body}
        </Box>
      )}
      {booting ? null : <Dock session={session} controller={controller} holding={holding || open} face={face} shift={shift} />}
    </Box>
  );
}
#+END_SRC

The spine owns the input while a step is open (=holding || open= on the dock), the way a held component owns the slot. =ctrl-o= takes the comp's =ctrl-m=, which a terminal sends as ⏎ (=** the comp=). The chrome is =rows − 1= tall, never =stdout.rows= (=codemap/design.md=). While the gate runs (=--chrome= from zsh), the crown reads =◐ connecting= and the body is the boot — the same =Boot= the captured face paints.

The session verbs, ingested into the trajectory vector. Each reads =ctx.session=; typed outside a session it BOOTS one on =home= and runs once the gate completes — F21 RULED by the handoff doc (/"It never prints 'needs the shell'"/): the verb re-enters the trajectory as =home= with =then= carried, and =open()= hands the line to =session.more= after the seat (M11). A verb whose answer is data returns it, and the render middleware paints it into the slot through =Effect= (M3, =capture.cut.print=). The arbitration over terminals and modes is =belt/pick.js=, one match answering and many opening =Search= in the slot:

#+BEGIN_SRC js :tangle ../../systems/ghost/trajectories/session/index.js :mkdirp yes
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { pick } from "../../belt/index.js";
import { Terminals, label, seat } from "../faces/Terminals.jsx";

const inside = (verb) => async (ctx) => {
  if (!ctx.session) {
    ctx.effect = await ctx.call(["home"], { then: ctx.signal.argv.join(" ") });
    ctx.rendered = true;
    return;
  }
  ctx.effect = await verb(ctx.session, ctx.signal.params, ctx);
};

const terminalLens = (session) => ({
  label: "terminal",
  rows: session.held.terminals.entities.map((terminal) => ({ label: label(terminal), seat: seat(terminal), id: terminal.id })),
  keys: ["label", "seat"],
  columns: ["label", "seat"],
  identifier: (row) => row.label,
});

const modeLens = (session) => ({
  label: "mode",
  rows: session.rows(),
  keys: ["daemon", "type", "slug"],
  columns: ["daemon", "type", "slug"],
  identifier: (row) => `${row.daemon}/${row.type}/${row.slug}`,
});

const held = (session, id) => session.held.terminals.entities.find((terminal) => terminal.id === id);

const buffers = (session) => {
  const thread = session.thread;
  const all = thread?.$buffers?.get() ?? [];
  const active = session.terminal.$buffer?.get();
  return all.map((buffer, at) => ({ "#": at + 1, label: buffer.label ?? "—", state: buffer === active ? "ACTIVE" : "—", fields: Object.keys(buffer.data).length }));
};

export const verbs = {
  terminals: inside(async (session, _, ctx) => {
    if (session.capture.face === "chrome") return (session.$step.set("terminal"), { stepped: "terminal" });
    await ctx.view.cut.hold({ session }, Terminals);
    return { active: session.terminal.id };
  }),
  new: inside(async (session, [mode, ...yap], ctx) => {
    await session.open();
    if (!mode) return (session.capture.shift("chrome"), session.$step.set("mode"), { stepped: "mode" });
    const chosen = await pick(ctx, modeLens(session), mode);
    if (!chosen?.row) return { error: `${mode}: no mode matches — viva mode/list` };
    return session.seat(chosen.row, yap.join(" "));
  }),
  use: inside(async (session, [token], ctx) => {
    const chosen = await pick(ctx, terminalLens(session), token ?? "");
    if (!chosen?.row) return chosen?.aborted ? chosen : { error: `${token}: no terminal matches — viva terminals` };
    return session.activate(held(session, chosen.row.id));
  }),
  close: inside(async (session, [token], ctx) => {
    const chosen = token ? await pick(ctx, terminalLens(session), token) : { row: { id: session.terminal.id } };
    if (!chosen?.row) return { error: `${token}: no terminal matches — viva terminals` };
    return session.drop(held(session, chosen.row.id));
  }),
  rename: inside(async (session, words) => {
    if (!words.length) return { error: "terminal/rename <label>" };
    const terminal = session.terminal;
    const traits = terminal.traits.includes("LABELED") ? terminal.traits : [...terminal.traits, "LABELED"];
    await terminal.write({ traits, trait: { ...terminal.trait, LABELED: words.join(" ") } });
    return { renamed: terminal.id, label: words.join(" ") };
  }),
  set: inside(async (session, [mode, ...yap], ctx) => {
    if (!mode) return (session.capture.shift("chrome"), session.$step.set("mode"), { stepped: "mode" });
    const chosen = await pick(ctx, modeLens(session), mode);
    if (!chosen?.row) return chosen?.aborted ? chosen : { error: `${mode}: no mode matches — viva mode/list` };
    return session.seat(chosen.row, yap.join(" "));
  }),
  list: inside((session) => ({ modes: session.rows().map((row) => ({ ...row, seated: session.seated?.slug === row.slug && session.seated?.daemon === row.daemon ? "ACTIVE" : "—" })) })),
  chat: inside((session, words) => session.turn(words.join(" "))),
  buffers: inside((session) => ({ buffers: buffers(session) })),
  buffer: inside((session) => ({ buffer: session.terminal.$buffer?.get()?.data ?? null })),
  push: inside(async (session) => {
    const thread = session.thread;
    if (!thread) return { error: "no thread is seated — viva mode/set" };
    session.terminal.$buffer.set(await thread.daemon.entities.buffer.create({ mode: thread.mode?.id ?? thread.mode, thread: thread.id, data: {} }));
    return { buffers: buffers(session) };
  }),
  pull: inside((session, [index]) => {
    const target = session.thread?.$buffers?.get()?.[Number(index) - 1];
    if (!target) return { error: `no buffer #${index ?? "?"} — viva buffers` };
    session.terminal.$buffer.set(target);
    return { buffers: buffers(session) };
  }),
  write: inside(async (session, [path, ...value]) => {
    const buffer = session.terminal.$buffer?.get();
    if (!buffer || !path || !value.length) return { error: "buffer/set <path> <value>" };
    const data = { ...buffer.data, [path]: value.join(" ") };
    await session.thread.daemon.entities.buffer.updateOne({ id: buffer.id }, { data });
    return { buffer: data };
  }),
  drop: inside(async (session, [index]) => {
    const all = session.thread?.$buffers?.get() ?? [];
    const target = index ? all[Number(index) - 1] : session.terminal.$buffer?.get();
    if (!target) return { error: `no buffer #${index ?? "?"} — viva buffers` };
    if (all.length === 1) return { error: "a thread keeps one buffer" };
    await session.thread.daemon.entities.buffer.removeOne({ id: target.id });
    if (target === session.terminal.$buffer.get()) session.terminal.$buffer.set(all.find((buffer) => buffer !== target));
    return { buffers: buffers(session) };
  }),
  chrome: inside((session) => (session.capture.shift("chrome"), { face: "chrome" })),
};

const words = v.object({ words: v.string().desc("the rest of the line").optional() });
const token = (name) => v.object({ [name]: v.string().optional() });

export function session(trajectory) {
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/terminals", valence: "every terminal on the lighthouse — the table captured, the spine in chrome" }, verbs.terminals);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/terminal/new", valence: "open a terminal — a mode seats it, the rest of the line is its first turn; no mode opens the spine's mode step", schema: v.object({ mode: v.string().optional(), yap: v.string().optional() }) }, verbs.new);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/terminal/use", valence: "activate a terminal by label or prefix — ambiguous opens the picker", schema: token("label") }, verbs.use);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/terminal/close", valence: "close a terminal — the active one by default; the thread stays on the lighthouse", schema: token("label") }, verbs.close);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/terminal/rename", valence: "relabel the active terminal", schema: words }, verbs.rename);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/mode/set", valence: "reseat the active terminal on a mode — a new thread; a bare mode slug does the same", schema: v.object({ mode: v.string().optional(), yap: v.string().optional() }) }, verbs.set);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/mode/list", valence: "every mode the daemons carry, grouped by daemon" }, verbs.list);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/chat", valence: "send a turn to the seated thread — any sentence does; chat forces it when the first word is a verb", schema: words }, verbs.chat);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffers", valence: "the seated thread's buffers — index, label, which is ACTIVE" }, verbs.buffers);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffer", valence: "the ACTIVE buffer's data, through Effect until a TUI view kind exists" }, verbs.buffer);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffer/new", valence: "push a buffer onto the thread — it becomes ACTIVE" }, verbs.push);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffer/use", valence: "activate a buffer by index", schema: token("index") }, verbs.pull);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffer/set", valence: "write one field of the ACTIVE buffer's data — the mode sees it next turn", schema: v.object({ path: v.string().optional(), value: v.string().optional() }) }, verbs.write);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/buffer/close", valence: "drop a buffer — the ACTIVE one by default; a thread keeps one", schema: token("index") }, verbs.drop);
  trajectory.open({ nature: "/chrome", valence: "the session's fullscreen face — ctrl-f and esc come back" }, verbs.chrome);
}
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC diff
// systems/ghost/trajectories/index.js:7
 import { census, flagged } from "./help.js";
 import { Help } from "./Help.jsx";
+import { session } from "./session/index.js";
…
   env: v.string().desc("<path>").examples("./environment/.env").optional(),
+  chrome: v.boolean().desc("open the session in its chrome — fullscreen").optional(),
 });
…
   trajectory.branch("/instances", instances);
+  session(trajectory);
#+END_SRC

Order binds. =session(trajectory)= runs inside the default export, before M4's references open their mode slugs at mount, so =chat · terminals · buffers · buffer · chrome= are literals already in the trie when =nature()= asks =trajectory.trie.has(row.slug)= (M4 pass 3): a mode named =chat= would live under its daemon. Inside a session the comp's =use · new · close= aliases are NOT opened (F22, OWED): three bare English words as root literals would shadow the yap remainder for any sentence that starts with them (=use the subjunctive here=); the handoff doc wants them (/"Inside the shell, use / new / close work as aliases"/). The noun form =terminal/use= is the address until beef rules. =terminals= in captured HOLDS the table (=cut.hold=): ⏎ or esc closes it and the dock's input returns; its answer is the active terminal's id, which the reference marks rendered — nothing paints twice. A bare mode slug reseats through M4's =reference= (=ctx.session= → =session.seat=), as the comp's =oracle ciao= does. =help= grows a =terminal · mode · buffer= group from the census, with no change to =help.js=.

** method

- Read whole this pass: =systems/ghost/mod.js= · =trajectories/{index,help}.js= · =belt/{pick,lens}.js= · the fourteen blocks above; =systems/runtime/run.js= · =lifecycle/process.js= · =lifecycle/{runtime,daemon,mode}/*.js= · =lifecycle/daemon/aperture/*.js= · every =lifecycle/mode/traits/*.js= · =gestalten/belt/stagger.js= · =prototypes/{daemon,runtime}.js=; =subsystems/typology/prototypes/{vector,signal,pattern,signature,controller,status,context,aperture}.js= · =gestalten/steer/{dispatch,match,strategy,trie}.js= · =gestalten/shape/{object,strip,tree,connection,http,mcp}.js= · =gestalten/belt/control.js= · =schematics/primitives/{controller,instance}.js=; =subsystems/sheets/mod.jsx= · =components/{Chrome,Background,Logo,Effect,Search}.jsx= · =theme.js=; anima =app/+layout.svelte= · =+page.svelte= · =bones/{crown,spine,shoulder}= · =panels/{a,b,c,g,h}= · =typology/entities/mode/{mode.js,traits/*}= · =entities/daemon/dossier.js= · =gestalten/belt/runner.js=; paladin =lifecycle/resolve.js= · =prototypes/ledger/integrity.js=; =~/.viva/registry/education/modes/home/aprende/{aprende.viva.js,aperture/index.js,tools/index.js}=; =.ikiro/connoisseur.md= whole.
- Two references written this pass by two opus agents at medium (the session's third and fourth launches — the fan-out token touched on beef's /"send off another agent"/, removed after): =.ikiro/reference/tui.md= — the terminal as a medium, the sheets kit, every ghost screen LIVE and DESIGNED, anima's chrome anatomy, the brief for the designer; =.ikiro/reference/ghost-architecture-comparison.md= — Emacs · Neovim · tmux (with nods to Blender, Acme, LSP, VS Code), the concept map, the two wirings of a mode, the chrome, the risks — 66 sources, 253 citations; gnu.org refused the fetches (429, then resets), so 27 Emacs sources were checked against the installed GNU Emacs 30.2 manuals and carry an (L) mark, and 4 claims (the three communities' regrets, VS Code's =engines=) are UNVERIFIED; every claim about us cites a line. Its §6 maps its 14 risks onto this pass and names two disagreements with =** held= below — read it, not this summary.
- The sandbox =work= tree of the pass above re-used for three probes; its =base= copy is still byte-equal to the live tree in every container this quest touches (anima's =bones/crown= and =panels/d= moved under a sibling since the copy — no m79 hunk touches either).
- The walk: connoisseur triggers 9 · 10 · 14–19 over every block; the three comparators over the structure; beef's two channels and the chrome as the questions.

- *1 · the doors.* Today a client learns a mode's doors from =/mode/<type>/<slug>/metadata/aperture= (=metadata.js:28= =shape.strip(mode.aperture)=) and anima's EXPOSED trait turns the strip into a callable =mode.call= (=anima entities/mode/traits/exposed.js:5= =shape.connection.wire=; M6 moves it to ghost's client, M10 wires it — the sandbox's =client.test.js= already reads =hello.modes.chaosmonkey.oracle.harness.dialogue.stream=). What no block does is put those doors on the TRAJECTORY: =references.open= hangs one literal per slug whose effect seats the mode, and the rest of the line is a turn. So =viva aprende board= sends "board" to aprende's harness as a sentence, and =viva help aprende= says "open education's home/aprende". Emacs's major mode contributes its commands to =M-x=; VS Code's extension declares =contributes.commands=; both are listed by the same =describe=/palette the core owns — our =census= (=trajectories/help.js:3=) IS that, and the strip IS the declaration. The rebuild is small: walk the strip and =mode.call= together, one edge per branch, an effect per leaf that calls the wired door — the runtime composed the path, the client still composes none:

#+BEGIN_SRC js
// systems/ghost/trajectories/references.js — F15 (b), sketched; not tangled
const doors = (edge, strip, call) => {
  for (const [segment, child] of Object.entries(strip.branches ?? {})) {
    const branch = edge.branch({ nature: segment, input: child.effect?.input, valence: child.effect?.valence ?? "" });
    if (child.effect) branch.affect((ctx) => call[segment](ctx.input));
    doors(branch, child, call[segment]);
  }
};
#+END_SRC

What it needs first: =edgeMeta= to carry the pattern's =valence= (finding 8) so =help= can say what a door does; the argv → input rule (positionals by the input schema's declared order, flags by name — the rule =Help.jsx:4-7= already prints); =shape.connection.wire= drops =*= and =(.*)= edges with a warn (=connection.js:7-9=), so a door behind a pattern does not reach a client today either. When: the client is live — inside a session always; headless, a reference with a tail hosts and connects (finding 15). The recipe's literals alone before that — the law's "opened at mount" holds for the slug, the doors are the daemon's word.
- *2 · the dock's grammar.* M11's =session.more(line)= dispatches every typed line from the root (=ctx.call(Signal.tokens(line), { session, shell })=); M12's placeholder reads /"a sentence · a command · a mode"/. The ranked =feed= makes a command or a reference win over the yap whenever the first word is an edge — so a chess student typing =play e4= to their tutor reseats the terminal on =board/play= (M4's own test, =references.test.js= "inside a session": =["play", "e4"]= → =seat play=), and =help me= runs =help= with the filter =me=. Every chat-first surface that also takes commands routes by a sigil: Slack and Discord =/=, Claude Code =/=, Emacs =M-x=, vim =:=. The shell argv is the other way round by nature — a command line, commands first, and =--= (probe 1) is its terminator. F16.
- *3 · the second channel.* A trait is a STATE word on the manifest, implemented =(mode, daemon) => finalizer= and staggered (=gestalten/belt/stagger.js:3-16=); the set is OPEN — an undeclared implementation is skipped, a declared one without an implementation too — and the client applies its own side the same way (=anima gestalten/belt/runner.js:1= =applyTraits=: =traitNamespace[trait]?.(entity, ctx)=). So a new trait degrades to nothing on an older ghost or an older runtime: the mechanism already fits beef's second channel. Three shapes, F17: a declarative face (data the kit renders — Blender draws its panels from RNA), a bundled ink sheet loaded by id (APPLICATION's twin: =application.js:23-33= bundles svelte through =paladin.bundler=; a =kind: "jsx"= with =react · ink · @vivalence/sheets= external, served under =/attached/bundle=, =import()=ed by ghost — Deno imports a URL), or none (doors through =Effect=, the harness through the Dock). The invariant /"nothing domain or mode specific can be loaded rendered or run on the client"/ (=invariants.md=) is kept by (a) and (b) and bent by (c) exactly as anima bends it for APPLICATION — server-bundled, loaded by id, domain words as data. "No leaving the mode" holds in all three: the face lives in the mode's directory.
- *4 · the version.* =/status= is the controller's =Status=, =/manifest= the mask's manifest — a daemon's carries the RECIPE's =version: "0.0.1"= (=commons/instances/hello-world/instance.viva.js:26=), never the code's; =/metadata/aperture= is a strip of edges with =input · output · yields · feeds · methods= (=strip.js:3-11=) and no level. Neovim ships =api_level · api_compatible · deprecated_since= in =nvim_get_api_info= and a UI refuses or adapts; LSP negotiates capabilities at =initialize=. Ghost will be installed apart from the runtime it joins (=install.sh=; an embedded runtime outlives every ghost that started it), so the skew is certain. One field on the runtime's =/metadata/manifest= — the package version and an integer wire level the strips bump — and =client()= (M10) compares. F18.
- *5 · =host({ start })=.* =host()= already computes the three states — a lock ALIVE → joined; =/status= RUNNING at the reach → joined; a lock that is not ALIVE → refused; nothing → launch — and then branches on a flag the caller passed to say whether it may start. The connoisseur's stop-tell (/"an options bag growing on a positional call"/): the mechanism answers =absent=; the verb decides. =host()= answering ={ launch: "absent" }= on an embedded recipe with nothing running lets =instance/lighthouse= say =viva instance/start= and lets =session.open= call =ctx.call(["instance/start", "runtime"])= and host again. Four files move (=host.js= · =lighthouse.js= · =session.js= · =host.test.js=), so F19, not a probe.
- *6 · the chrome.* Below, under its own heading.
- *7 · the recipe, not the roster.* =references.rows= reads =daemon.kernel[].reference= off the mounted recipe (a seated entry is ={ module, mountpoint, reference, url, bundles }=, =resolve.js:85-101= — no manifest unless the module is inline). The daemon's =/metadata/modes= is the truth (=type · slug · name · traits=) but needs the wire; the registry's pensieve has the same manifest on disk (=registry.js:207= =accio(module)= → =manifest.traits · name · description=). Chess seats four topologies and two topographies beside five modes (=world/ledger.md=): =viva openings= opens a thread on a DATASET-only mode and the first turn answers =no harnessed thread is seated=. An edge a terminal can face is one whose traits say so (HARNESSED today; APPLICATION once M13 renders buffers); =accio= answers that at the pinhole without the wire. QUESTIONED: whether a data-only mode deserves an edge at all (beef: /"open the whatever that represents"/).
- *8 · two missing words.* =/metadata/modes= gives =name= and =traits= but not =manifest.description= (aprende carries one, =aprende.viva.js:13=); =edgeMeta= plucks =input · output · yields · feeds · methods= and drops the pattern's =valence= — the one line a palette shows. Two one-line additions on the runtime and typology side; both land with F15.
- *9 · F13's copy.* ={ ...signal, type }= is a plain object: =nature · trace · gauges= and a fin's =params · flags= come along, the Signature prototype does not — =step.absolute=, =step.hash=, =step.json=, =step.fin= are undefined on every step from M2 on. No reader in 253 + 73 files touched them (the sandbox census), so the pass holds; the next reader of =steps[n].absolute= will not. Evidence for F13 (b) — =scope= carries the pattern as a fourth tuple element and =traverse= reads =pattern.type= — or for a copy that keeps the class (=Object.assign(new signal.constructor(signal.nature), …)= re-coerces; =Object.create(signal)= trips the private =#hash=). beef's, as F13 already is.
- *10 · =Session= and the dock.* =Session= (M11) holds =hosting · held · terminal= (the container — anima's =+layout=) and also =$live · $echo · $error= and =turn()= (the dock's — anima's =Dock.svelte:157-184= keeps =live= and =echo= in the widget). M13's terminals table and a buffer in a frame will want the dock to be one body among others; the stores that belong to a dock should travel with =Dock.jsx=. Lean: =Session= keeps =seat · more · close=; =turn= and its three atoms move into the Dock. Not a probe — M12 is designed, not run.
- *11 · =live= vs focus.* The shell passes a boolean so the Dock's =useInput= stands down while a picker holds the slot (M3: /"every active useInput hears every key"/). ink has a focus system — =useFocus()= · =useFocusManager()= are exported by the kit (=sheets/mod.jsx:18-38=); a painted component taking focus and the Dock reading =isFocused= is the mechanism the connoisseur asks for (/"the mechanism IS the guard"/). ink cycles focus on Tab by default (=enableFocus= / =disableFocus=), which a text input dislikes — QUESTIONED until a walk with both.
- *12 · 13 · 14* — the three probes, below.
- *15 · headless references.* =viva --json oracle hi= answers ={ route: "reference", receiver, payload }= and exits; the quest says so (/"Until the chat lands, a reference or a yap in a pipe resolves to its Resolution"/). Neovim's =--headless= runs the same commands with no UI; our law 9 says every verb is reachable without a TTY. The successor: a headless reference with a tail hosts, connects, seats and prints the turn's packets as JSON lines (=soma.scan= is the fold) — named under =* deferred= now.
- *16 · detach and attach.* /(10-06: inline · embedded are NOT beef's render names — embedded is only the runtime's launch word, F7; the render names are cut · captured · chrome, the law)/ beef's two words are tmux's two modes: inline is a server that dies with its one client, embedded is =tmux='s server — =instance/start='s supervisor survives every ghost and the next =viva home= joins it (M8). What does not survive is the SEAT: =session.close= removes the terminal row (M11), anima keeps its rows and re-opens on the active one. A returning ghost starts on nothing — F11's per-session row is the cause; the table (M13) is the cure.
- *17 · the echo area.* Emacs prints every message to the echo area and keeps them in =*Messages*=; the inline runtime's taps (=[hal …]=, =[migrator]=, =[probe]=) print through =console= over ink's frame (the deferred item measures five lines on one login). The runtime's =stdout= is a span (=run.js:15= taps it to the console) and =integration.ledger= already writes every record to =<ledger>/logs/<slug>/spans.jsonl= — the chrome's shoulder row is where those records belong when a shell holds the screen, =console= silent. Amended under deferred.
- *18 · =shape.mcp=.* =gestalten/shape/mcp.js:6= folds a Vector into =tools/list= · =tools/call= over JSON-RPC with =initialize=; =shape.mcp(trajectory)= is the old quest's third layer in one line. Amended under deferred.
- *19 · the client builds a path.* The comparison agent's first finding, confirmed on the tree: =ModeDossier= (=anima entities/mode/mode.js:62-63=) composes =ctx.daemon.reference.branch(`/mode/${type}/${slug}`)= and the matching =connection= itself, while =/metadata/modes= already answers each mode's =metadata= path under the daemon (=metadata.js:20=) and the ORM row carries =type · slug= only. Law 5 — /"the runtime composes every path, a client composes none"/ — is broken on the one seam M6 moves whole into =@vivalence/ghost/client=. The fix is the row's word: the mode's =reference= on the =/metadata/modes= row (or the Mode entity) and the dossier branching on what it reads. One hunk on the runtime, one on the dossier; a M6 amendment on go.

** the chrome — what the viva chrome in the CLI is

beef: /"whats the viva chrome in the cli?? about the tui chrome."/ · /"we might want to be able to render different chrome in the effects no? repo provides, but other components/modes can supply their own."/ · /"i guess chrome might be how we name the custom interactive fullscreen components."/

Today =Chrome= is a padded box with the logo (=sheets/components/Chrome.jsx:6-13=), and M12's =Dock.jsx= paints the status row, the transcript, the streaming turn, the error line, the slot, the prompt and the key hints in one component. Forty years of terminal programs agree on the rows a chrome holds — Emacs's mode line · echo area · minibuffer, tmux's status line, Neovim's statusline · cmdline · messages — and anima already names the bones. The proposal maps them; every cell past LIVE is QUESTIONED, the designer's to draw (=.ikiro/reference/tui.md= §6 · §9):

| slot | row | holds | anima | state |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| crown | bottom while inline (ink's =Static= commits every turn ABOVE the live area, so the status row lands under the transcript, over the prompt — tmux's status line); top only under the alternate screen (F12) | instance · identity · launch (inline · joined) · daemon › mode (the seat) · connection standing | =bones/crown= (the terminals) · =bones/spine= (lighthouse standing · activity pips) | DESIGNED as the Dock's first line (M12) — the M12 walk shows it FIRST, which inline cannot do (=tui.md= §6, rendered) |
| stage | middle, grows | the seated buffer — the transcript (=Static=, the scrollback) today; a mode buffer later | =panels/a= (=Frame= → buffer view · Dock) | DESIGNED (M12) · M13 |
| slot | inside the stage | where a typed command paints (=instance/doctor= above the prompt) | =panels/c= panes (terminals · mode · buffer · harness) | DESIGNED (M3 =Slot= · M12 ={slot}=) |
| shoulder | one line above the prompt | the echo area: =● streaming=, an error, a one-line effect, the runtime's taps (finding 17) | =bones/shoulder= (Phase · Harness · Dock toggles) | DESIGNED in part (M12 error + streaming lines) |
| prompt | bottom | the input line — a turn; =/= + the trajectory (F16) | the Dock's composer (=panels/a/widgets/Dock.svelte=) | DESIGNED (=TextInput=, M12) |
| hints | last row | the keys that count NOW, contextual (which-key · tmux status-right) | — | DESIGNED as one static line (M12) |
| pincer | a key | opens the trajectory as a picker over =census= — the palette (Emacs =M-x= · tmux prefix) | =bones/pincer= (the radial) | QUESTIONED |

- /(10-06: superseded by the comp — =view.capture(data, { captured, chrome })=, the faces in M12 · M13; the slots below are the crown · spine · stage · transcript · dock there)/ The shape: =Chrome= takes the slots — =view.shell(data, Chrome, Body)= stays, but =Chrome= renders =crown · stage · shoulder · prompt · hints= and the Body fills the stage; the session paints crown and shoulder from what it holds (host · client · terminal), the Dock paints the stage and owns the prompt while a harness is seated, a mode's face (F17) paints the stage otherwise. /"repo provides, but other components/modes can supply their own"/ → the repo's =Chrome= is the default; a mode's face replaces the stage, never the crown.
- Colour and ground: the kit is 16 ANSI names (=sheets/theme.js=; its =brand= magenta is a colour no dapper theme uses), the four dapper themes are hexes, and ink 5 cannot fill a box with a background — the terminal's own background is the ground of every screen, unknown to the program. A truecolor mapping is the designer's call (=tui.md= §7, both options measured); the slots above hold at 80 columns.
- Found by the renders (=tui.md= §2–3), outside this quest: =trajectories/instance/Init.jsx:137,212= pass =instance=…= where =Logo= and =Banner= read =variant= — the wizard shows the plain wordmark and a cyan "instance up" (known issue); the kit pads columns by string length, so a Japanese or Chinese cell misaligns every table (known issue); =--buffer= without a TTY dies on ink's raw-mode error today — M3's =own= gate (=ctx.interactive=) closes it; a flag before the verb exits 127 today — M1 closes it.
- Not decided here: what persists to scrollback on esc (the transcript, per F12 inline); how a streaming turn paints (=Static= is append-only, the live turn re-renders below it); how tool calls show (=▸ name= today); light terminals.

** modes into ghost — the two channels

beef: /"we will wire mode into the ghost both via runtime/daemon and directly with specific capabilities offered via mode traits specifically to aide ghost."/

- *Through the daemon — what exists.* =/metadata/modes= (=metadata.js:14-22=: =type · slug · name · traits · metadata=); per mode =/metadata/{manifest,aperture,statics,mountpoint}= always and =application · emitter · freight · harness= by trait (=:24-49=); =shape.connection.wire= inflates each strip into =mode.call · mode.emit · mode.harness= on the client (anima's traits, ghost's after M6 · M10). Missing for a CLI: the TRAJECTORY edge per door (F15), =description= and =valence= on the wire (finding 8), a version (F18). The law holds throughout: the runtime composed every path the strip names; a grafted door calls what =wire= built.
- *Through traits — what the mechanism allows.* A trait is a declared state; the runtime staggers the ones it implements and skips the rest (=stagger.js:6=), the client applies the ones it knows (=runner.js:5=). A ghost-facing trait degrades to nothing on either older side — the safest extension point the system has. Its export is the question (F17): data (a declarative face), code (a bundled sheet), or none. Blender draws panels from data; Emacs loads code; VS Code declares in a manifest and loads code lazily. The house precedent is APPLICATION — code, bundled by the runtime, loaded by id, domain-blind by construction. /"if its concrete, its a literal"/ leans the first step to (a): the data a door already carries (its =input= schema with its examples) renders through =Effect= and the kit's =Form= before any mode ships a view.
- *Degradation table.*

| ghost ↔ runtime | F15 doors | F17 trait | F18 version |
|---|---|---|---|
| same | edges under the literal | the face renders | equal — silent |
| ghost newer | the strip lacks =valence= → edges without a line | the trait's export missing → skipped | lower → ghost warns, every door still works |
| runtime newer | unknown edge meta ignored | unknown trait → skipped on the client | higher → ghost names the gap, offers =viva registry/…= |

** three probes landed in the sandbox

The =work= tree (M1–M14 applied) took three small changes, each carried into the blocks above; run from that tree, one process: =deno test --config deno.jsonc -A --no-check systems/ghost/tests/signal.test.js subsystems/typology/tests/signal.test.js subsystems/typology/tests/signal.snapshot.test.js systems/ghost/tests/references.test.js systems/ghost/tests/pick.test.js= → =ok | 38 passed (4 steps) | 0 failed (1s)= — =signal= 21 (was 20) · =references= 8 (was 6) · =pick= 7 · typology =signal= + its snapshot unchanged.

- *Probe 1 · POSIX =--= (M1).* The argv row cuts the array at the first =--=: what follows is params, dashes and all; =argv= spells the terminator back when a param opens with =--=. =viva home -- instance use= sends =instance use=; =viva flashcards --fast -- --not-a-flag= has =flags { fast }= and =params ["--not-a-flag"]=; a bare =--= is nothing, not a flag named ="". The deferred item that recorded the hole is gone.
- *Probe 2 · the trie decides (M4).* =references.nature(trajectory, row, all)= asks =trajectory.trie.has(row.slug)= — a slug that is already an edge (a verb, a branch, the =home= literal, which now opens FIRST) or shared by two daemons lives under its daemon; the =VERBS= set is gone. A mode slugged =instance= beside the =instance= branch lands at =edu/instance=, never on the branch's effect.
- *Probe 3 · children before fuzz (M4).* =yap.nearest(trajectory, word)= answers the trie's children when the word is an edge (=natures(trajectory, word)= — the lens narrowed to =word/=), the fuzzy search otherwise; the picker opens on those children with an empty query. Walked headless on the sandbox: =viva instance= → =near: instance/use  instance/create  instance/init  instance/run  instance/start  instance/stop  instance/delete  instance/rename  instance/lighthouse  instance/doctor=, exit 127 (the fuzzy list had =instances/*= in it); =viva registry= → its five; =viva instanc= — no edge — still searches, =instances/*= first.

Replay: the blocks above through =apply-diffs.py= onto a fresh copy of =base=, then =diff -rq= against =work= — the line is pasted under =* changelog=.

** held

- M2's ranked =feed= — Emacs's keymap precedence is a stack (overriding → minor → local → global), ours a rank by segment type; both say the specific beats the general and both leave declaration order out of it. Clean for ONE source. The comparison (§6) objects: Emacs ranks WHO bound a key, Neovim keeps user commands apart from built-ins by case; F15 and F16 add the modes as a second source to the trie, and a collision today is settled by renaming the reference under its daemon (probe 2) — a rule of segment types, not of sources. Held, with that objection standing until F15 rules.
- /"Hosting changes the transport, never the client"/ (law 5) is Neovim's UI/core cut and anima's dogma in one sentence; =reach= as the one seam (M6) keeps anima byte-equal. Clean.
- =instance/doctor= is =:checkhealth=; =--json= on every verb is =--headless= parity; the Controller machine is the one lifecycle under buffers, activities, views and the runtime (=schematics/primitives/controller.js:3-23=) — one structure, visible. Clean.
- The sheets kit on =@vivalence/typology= (the =Controller=, F10) — the comparison agent's precedent: Neovim's UI speaks the core's protocol, it does not avoid it. Held, F10 stays beef's.
- Scale: the trajectory is rebuilt per process from the recipe (+ the strips under F15) — linear in doors; =census= is one fold. Nothing here grows faster than the instance.

- *M1 · the path.* A leading =--flag= became the path (=viva --json= → a segment named =--json=) while the quest's own test expected =null=. Now the path is the first token not opening =--=.
- *M1 · =Signal.tokens=.* It answered the string tokenizer's =--k=v= split, which the argv row then read as a boolean and a param. Now =words()= — the quote-aware split alone.
- *M1 · the =.absolute= readers.* =mod.js:31,145= read =signal.absolute=, argv on =ShellSignal=, the natures on =Signal=. Now =argv=.
- *M1 · inner failures.* =ctx.call= answered =null= when the inner dispatch failed — the catch middleware parks the error where nothing reads it. Now ={ error }=.
- *M3 · no =ctx.view.shell=.* The session opened a shell the middleware never offered. Offered, refused by name inside a shell.
- *M3 · =--buffer=.* The effect painted after the shell's root had unmounted. It paints, then the shell holds.
- *M3 · ctrl-c.* ink exits on ctrl-c by default (=render.js:14=): a =render= hung on a component ink had unmounted, and the shell's body never heard the key. ink's exit now kills a render's controller; the shell turns it off.
- *M3 · keys.* Every active =useInput= hears every key — esc in a painted picker also left the session. The shell tells its body =live=.
- *M3 · =JsonTree=.* Still =buffer?.release?.()=, a form the gate's grep missed. Moved to the controller; the gate catches the form.
- *M4 · the pre-mount.* The pinhole mounted before every verb; =instance/doctor <other>= then read the pre-mounted instance (the memo keys by object) with the first =.env= still claimed. Only a first word that is no command, or =help=, mounts.
- *M4 · the yap's picker listed the yap.* =help.js= names the remainder =<sentence>=; the filter looked for =(.*)=.
- *M4 · inside a session* a reference or a sentence painted its own effect into the slot. They mark =ctx.rendered=.
- *M4 · prose* contradicted the M11 stub.
- *M5 · the recipe's only mode* was the hello-world demo, whose every route answers 500 on an OPEN known issue (=hello-world-mode-hook-reads-ctx-user-before-authorize=) — the client would refuse the daemon, M10's test and M12's walk with it. The chaosmonkey oracle and its vision until the issue closes.
- *M6 · the hydrate effect* wrote a row of two empty maps at once (nanostores runs it on registration; =map({})= is truthy) and half rows mid-login — on the ledger's store a fault, as an unhandled rejection. A content predicate, a span fault; the ledger skips half rows (M9).
- *M6 · orphans.* The move left anima's =prototypes/index.js · stores/index.js · gestalten/= re-exporting moved files. Deleted in M6, =conversation.js= with them (was M14).
- *M6 · the barrel's top level* lost =bridge · box · lighthouse · terminals=. No importer uses them (grep at the fold: 0); =codemap/anima.md= names the form; kept.
- *M6 · the purity gate* grepped =client/= whole and went red on its own seam. Now the structures, the two seams named.
- *M7 · the abort.* The first draft's one line could not carry it: a typology =Request= drops a caller's =signal= (=request.js:5-24=), and =Response.stream= has no =cancel=. =cancel= returns the iterator; the dropped signal is a known issue.
- *M7 · =/echo=* had arity 1 — it receives the context.
- *M8 · a BOOTING row* was overwritten. Refused by name.
- *M8 · embedded* started =specs("all")= — anima too, detached — and a login could leave an instance running. =instance/start runtime=; =start: false= for the login.
- *M8 · a failed inner start* joined a =null= lock. Thrown (M1's ={ error }=).
- *M8 · the runtime barrel* loaded for every =viva=. Imported inside =inline()=.
- *M8 · M10 · =client.test.js= imported =host.test.js=* — Deno registers that file's tests too. =tests/rig.js=.
- *M8 · M10 · sanitizers.* The shutdown beacon and the client's subscriptions outlive an in-process boot. A named opt-out.
- *M8 · =localhost=* binds =[::1]=, =fetch= picks =127.0.0.1=. The rig uses =127.0.0.1=.
- *M9 · =Card.slug= as =Slug=* refused a username a login had just accepted. A plain string.
- *M9 · the file was born =0644=.* =0600= at creation.
- *M10 · a =null= channel* threw on the first daemon request (=Span.to(null)=). The client logger's channel.
- *M10 · the deadline timer* lived 30 s past the race. Cleared.
- *M10 · =origin= was the decoded =Url=* — ink cannot render it, =String()= prints =[object Object]=. The address string.
- *M11 · a seat that throws* after the shell paints left ink mounted under =Deno.exit= — raw mode. The shell is killed first.
- *M11 · ghost never exited after esc* — the client's subscriptions loop forever. =Deno.exit(0)= ends a successful dispatch.

The in-process files ran together in one process per file and passed: host · client · session boot the runtime up to twice in one process (hazard 15 did not fire).

* QA

** ① testimony
- I type =viva instance/lighthouse login beef ****= on a cold localhost with =launch: inline= — the runtime boots in my shell, the card prints with =stored: ~/.viva/credentials.json=, the runtime stops. New terminal, =viva instance/doctor= — the =authority= row reads =localhost · home: null=. No second login.
- I type =viva home what did i get wrong yesterday= with =HOMED= set on my row — the chrome opens inline, the first line reads =inline · http://localhost:2503/… · localhost · hello › chaosmonkey/oracle=, my sentence sits under =you=, the oracle's answer streams under =assistant=, short and in character, =● streaming  ctrl-c stop= pulses. =viva instances/list= from another shell shows =running: ghost=. I press esc — the transcript stays in scrollback, the list shows nothing running.
- Same with the instance ALIVE from =viva instance/start= — no second boot; the first line says =joined=.
- Inside the dock I type =instance/doctor= — it renders above the input line, the chat stays. I type =oracle hi there= — the dock reseats on a new oracle thread and =hi there= streams. I type =vision= — it reseats, and a sentence there answers =no harnessed thread is seated=. I type a sentence — a turn on whatever is seated.
- I type =viva --json home hi= — the Resolution as JSON (={ "route": "reference", "receiver": {…}, "payload": "hi" }=), no frame, exit 0. =viva home hi | cat= — the same.
- =viva instanc= — the picker preset to =instanc= over the =instance/*= and =instances/*= natures; =viva visio= — one match, the dock opens on =vision= without a picker; =viva nope deeper= — a sentence: the choice screen; =viva instance/nope= — =ghost: no handler for instance/nope= · =near: instance/use  instance/create …=, exit 127 /(pass: walked headless; the first draft's line named =instance nope= and =instances/*= among the near)/.
- Without a =HOMED= row there is no =home= edge: =viva home hi= is a sentence, and the yap screen offers only the search and names the missing trait. With a =HOMED= mode the instance does not seat, =viva home= says =home: … is not a mode of this instance=.

* deferred
- buffers rendered in the ghost terminal (deliverable n, M13) — /"that comes later"/; reopens after M12 lands.
- a writer for =identity.trait.HOMED= — no lighthouse route updates an identity's traits today (=attachment/entities.js= walls =/daemon= and =/terminal= only); until one exists the row is edited by hand; reopens with the first identity-trait verb (m60's territory).
- a mode's contribution to the client while seated (F17 (b)): one trait, one Vector export stripped onto =/mode/<t>/<s>/metadata/<export>= and wired by the client — =/keys= · =/palette= · =/turn= · =/sheet= · =/hints=, layered ghost < domain < mode < the operator's own; beef widened it 10-06 (/"customize keybindings, colors, harnesses, whatever"/) and parked it (/"we dont need that now. start withuot."/). Reopens at the first mode the dock and =Effect= cannot serve; the noun (=helm= · =HELMED= free) is his.
- F8 /"open as turn"/ as something other than /"send to $home"/ — both dispatch =home <words>= until beef names what a stashed turn is.
- MCP stdio / agent JSON surface — the old ghost quest's third layer; the mechanism exists: =shape.mcp(vector)= (=T/gestalten/shape/mcp.js:6=) folds a Vector into =tools/list= · =tools/call= over JSON-RPC, so =shape.mcp(trajectory)= is one line when an agent needs ghost; reopens then.
- the harness route's missing bearer check (gap 2) and the arming of =paladin.skills.{fs,shell}= with the host's permissions (hazard 25) — m60's.
- two instances in one ghost process — the paladin singleton forbids it; reopens only with a paladin redesign.
- credentials rotation · several identities per origin — at the first second account.
- a GHOST trait on the Terminal row (F11) — an enum edit on a lighthouse entity → waits on m78 or road A; the row is removed on exit instead, so anima never keeps a dead one.
- a view test that sends keys — the shell, the Dock, a picker; the input-free half is tested (=view.render.test.js=). Reopens with a fake stdin the kit grows.
- the anima dev watcher: =vite.config.mjs:37-41= =watch.include= does not name =systems/ghost/client=; vite watches an outside-root module it serves on its own — unproven. Reopens at the first walk that edits a client file under =anima/watch= and sees no reload.
- the lock is read-then-written by the host as by =Die.resolve= (=die.js:43-51=) — two processes in the same millisecond both see no row; reopens with an exclusive create (=Deno.open(…, { createNew: true })=) for both, a paladin change.
- an inline runtime's console taps (=[hal …]=, the multiplex upgrade line, =[probe] retry=) print into the shell above ink's frame — noise; reopens when the runtime's taps move to its span (=runtime.md=: the one console tap is =Activity.ts:18=). Measured on the login walk: two =[migrator]= lines, two =[instance] … hallucinators[n] filtered … empty key= lines, and one =[probe …] fetch failed …/status= from the host's own cold probe. The chrome's answer is an echo area (architecture pass, finding 17): the runtime's =stdout= span records tapped into the shoulder row and the ledger's =spans.jsonl=, =console= silent under a shell.
- =sheets= now depends on =@vivalence/typology= (the =Controller=, F10) — a new edge in the design kit; reopens if beef rules the kit stays typology-free.
- a one-word miss opens the picker; a sentence whose first word is a near nature (=flashc teach me=) goes to the choice screen, not to =flashcards= with =teach me= — the address study's prefix road (=address.test.js= "prefix of ONE edge descends") is not built; reopens at the first walk that wants it.

* release
- added `VIVA_CLIENT_GHOST_CREDENTIALS_FILE` · `--credentials=<path>` — ghost keeps lighthouse authority at `<ledger>/credentials.json` by default, mode 0600, one row per lighthouse origin · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `viva instance/lighthouse` — `signup | login` persist the authority; `logout` added; `password` (the operator reset) kept; reaches a cold inline instance by hosting it for the call · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `viva <sentence>` in a pipe — exits 1 naming the way out (`viva home …` when a HOMED mode exists, else `try: viva help`); was a 127 NotFound on its first word · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `@vivalence/runtime/scenarios` `lighthouse.create` — takes `{ module }`, `@commons/lighthouse/multiplayer` by default · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- added `runtime.statics.launch` (`inline | embedded`) on the instance/ledger recipe — how a client starts a runtime it does not find · migrate: none, default `embedded` ⟨m79⟩
- added `@commons/lighthouse/localhost` · `commons/instances/localhost` — one identity, every signup and login answers it · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `Response.stream` — a reader that cancels returns the source iterator: a client that stops reading ends the server's generator · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `shard.transmitter.inline` — the request's own signal rides the in-process `Request` (a timeout's `abort()` reaches the server) · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `viva <verb>` chaining — a failing inner verb (`instance/use x run`) fails the outer one with its message, exit 1; it answered `null`, exit 0 · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- moved anima's client structures (`stores/lighthouse · terminals`, `entities/*`, `Dataspace · Cargo · Entity`, `chain · narrow · applyTraits`, `logger`, `dock`) to `@vivalence/ghost/client` · migrate: anima imports flipped in the same change; `hydrate(lighthouse, store)` takes a store and is async ⟨m79⟩
- added `viva home <sentence>` · `viva <mode-slug> [sentence]` · `viva <sentence>` — the shell, a terminal over the lighthouse; a one-word miss opens the picker · migrate: none ⟨m79⟩
- changed `Signal` — coerces an argv array; a bare `--` ends the flags (POSIX), what follows is params; `flags · params · argv` getters; `Signal.tokens(line)`; `ShellSignal · ShellContext` removed · migrate: `new ShellSignal(argv)` → `new Signal(argv)`; `signal.absolute` (argv) → `signal.argv` ⟨m79⟩
